4:00 PM · museums · Free · Museum of Modern Art · Midtown · Manhattan · Score 46.4  
MoMA offers free admission on Friday evenings. Free tickets are limited, released in advance, and go quickly.
**Timing:** Free Friday evenings, roughly 4-8pm. Confirm the current window and ticket release on moma.org.
**Practical note:** Book the free timed ticket the moment it is released; the line for walk-ups is long.
**Why now:** A standing weekly free window at a museum that is otherwise one of the most expensive in the city.

#### [Queens Night Market](https://queensnightmarket.com/)

4:00 PM · markets · Free entry; most dishes capped at a few dollars · Queens Night Market · Corona · Queens · Score 53.7  
Open-air night market behind the New York Hall of Science with about a hundred independent food, art, and merchandise vendors, deliberately kept low-priced.
**Timing:** Saturdays 4pm to midnight. Note the seasonal gap between late August and mid September.
**Practical note:** Outdoors and cash-friendly; the adjacent lot charges for parking, so the 7 train is easier.
**Why now:** Runs every Saturday evening in season; the 7 train to 111th St drops you four blocks away.

## Where to eat

Checked the risky dining claims against captured public evidence and limited follow-up public pages; kept useful current offers/events while downgrading ordinary menus and rejecting same-restaurant duplicates.

Dining research: 2026-10-06T10:30:46+00:00

No Michelin star claims were retained. The Kirbee's source mentions Michelin-related recognition for the operators' Texas restaurants, not an award for Kirbee's; that distinction should remain clear. Access limits: Motek happy-hour PDF text was unreadable, the Queens Farm TicketSpice page exposed only purchase-protection text, and the CB3 PDF was blocked.

### Good Time Country Buffet all-you-can-eat dinner

Deals & happy hours — Good Time Country Buffet

New buffet from the Kisa and C as in Charlie team with fried chicken, collards, mac and cheese, rotating dishes, salad bar, and unlimited trips through the line; useful as a comparatively affordable sit-down dinner.

Price: $26.99 adults; $23.99 seniors 65+; $13.99 kids ages 4-9; desserts and boozy frozen drinks extra  
Schedule: Wednesday-Sunday, 5:00 PM-10:00 PM  
Conditions: Walk-ins plus a limited number of Resy reservations; sweet tea/soda, desserts, beer, and frozen drinks cost extra where stated; tax/tip/service charges not stated.  
From: 2026-09-12  
Opened: 2026-09-12  
Published: 2026-09-10  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/news/413774/good-time-country-buffet-east-village-opening-kisa)

### Fish Cheeks NoHo happy hour window

Deals & happy hours — Fish Cheeks

Official location metadata lists a daily happy-hour window at the original NoHo Thai seafood spot; exact happy-hour item pricing was not exposed in accessible text, so treat it as a timing lead to verify before going.

Schedule: Happy hour 12:00 PM-5:00 PM; restaurant hours Sunday-Thursday 12:00 PM-10:15 PM, Friday-Saturday 12:00 PM-11:15 PM  
Conditions: Official Fish Cheeks structured location data confirms the NoHo happy-hour window but not item prices or exclusions; verify current happy-hour items day-of. Source: https://www.fishcheeksnyc.com/menus/  
[Source](https://www.fishcheeksnyc.com/menus/)

### Fish Cheeks Williamsburg happy hour window

Deals & happy hours — Fish Cheeks Williamsburg

Brooklyn branch has a longer official happy-hour window than NoHo, making it a useful Williamsburg Thai-seafood drinks/snacks target if the current item list checks out day-of.

Schedule: Happy hour every day 12:00 PM-6:00 PM; restaurant hours Sunday-Thursday 12:00 PM-10:15 PM, Friday-Saturday 12:00 PM-10:45 PM  
Conditions: Official Fish Cheeks structured location data confirms the Williamsburg happy-hour window but not item prices or exclusions; verify current happy-hour items day-of. Source: https://www.fishcheeksnyc.com/menus/  
[Source](https://www.fishcheeksnyc.com/menus/)

### Ci Siamo weekday aperitivo menu in the bar and lounge

Special menus — Ci Siamo

Official aperitivo menu offers a bounded weekday late-afternoon bar/lounge option with snacks such as mortadella, pizza bianca, pizzette, smashed potatoes, and focaccia.

Price: Aperitivo items shown at $14-$27 where priced; examples include mortadella $14, pizza bianca $15, pizzette $16, focaccia $18, and caramelized onion torta $27  
Schedule: Monday-Friday, 3:00 PM-4:30 PM, bar and lounge only  
Conditions: Official page says available in the bar and lounge; guests should inform team of food allergies; drinks, tax, tip, and service charges not specified for this menu.  
[Source](https://www.cisiamo.com/menus/)

### Manhatta weekend lunch prix fixe

Special menus — Manhatta

Official weekend lunch menu offers a fixed-price way into Manhatta with skyline views at lower cost than dinner, including choices like hamachi crudo, dry-aged burger, sea bass, duck confit, and desserts.

Price: Two courses $65; three courses $78; oysters show a $25 caviar add-on  
Schedule: Weekend lunch; exact service hours not stated in fetched menu text  
Conditions: Reservations likely needed; official fetched menu did not state tax/tip/service charges or blackout dates.  
[Source](https://www.manhattarestaurant.com/menus/)

### Ci Siamo Thanksgiving prix fixe

Special menus — Ci Siamo

Official holiday special is already posted: Italian-leaning Thanksgiving menu with focaccia, primi, secondi, and seasonal dishes; useful for planners willing to book beyond the next 30 days.

Price: $155 per person  
Schedule: Thanksgiving menu; exact seating times not stated in fetched text  
Conditions: Official page states the $155 Thanksgiving price includes the food menu; beverages, tax, and gratuity are additional, and the posted menu is a sample. Source: https://www.cisiamo.com/menus/  
Event date: 2026-11-26  
[Source](https://www.cisiamo.com/menus/)

### Manhatta Thanksgiving four-course menu

Special menus — Manhatta

Official Thanksgiving menu is posted for a higher-end holiday meal, with a four-course structure and optional white-truffle add-on.

Price: Four courses $225; Italian white truffle add-on or tagliolini middle course $95  
Schedule: Thanksgiving menu; exact seating times not stated in fetched text  
Conditions: Official page states the Thanksgiving menu is a sample and may vary; fetched text does not say whether beverages, tax, tip, or service are included. Source: https://www.manhattarestaurant.com/menus/  
Event date: 2026-11-26  
[Source](https://www.manhattarestaurant.com/menus/)

### Amazon-influenced Brazilian restaurant opens on the Lower East Side

New openings — iara

Former Theodora chef de cuisine Vitor Mendes opened a Brazilian open-kitchen restaurant using Amazonian ingredients; official menu lists pão de queijo, tacacá, moqueca, picanha, tucupi martini, caipirinha, and cupuaçu ice cream.

Price: Official menu page lists dishes without prices; Eater's opening report gives examples including dadinho de tapioca $16, moqueca $48, picanha con fritas $68, and farofa $6  
Schedule: Tuesday-Saturday, 5:00 PM-11:00 PM  
Conditions: Reservations through Resy; official page does not state tax/tip/service charges; opening date verified from Eater report, official site published Oct. 6 menu/press pages.  
From: 2026-10-02  
Opened: 2026-10-02  
Published: 2026-10-01  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/news/414079/iara-nyc-brazilian-restaurant-open-amazon-rainforest-ingredients-cassava-vitor-mendes)

### Cafe Charmant brings a coastal French brasserie to Bleecker Street

New openings — Cafe Charmant

Catch Hospitality opened a French brasserie in the former Saint Theo’s space, with chef Jake Tyler Brodsky and a menu leaning into French staples, raw bar, and seafood.

Schedule: Dinner Tuesday-Saturday, 5:00 PM-10:00 PM  
Conditions: Official homepage confirms address and dinner hours; the fetched official menus page exposed only a page shell, so item prices are not verified. Source: https://www.cafecharmantnyc.com/  
Published: 2026-09-04  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/news/413345/cafe-charmant-nyc-restaurant-opening-west-village-eugene-remm-the-corner-store-catch)

### Creola opens as a 14-seat Black diaspora tasting-menu supper club

New openings — Creola

Chef Lana Lagomarsini’s intimate restaurant centers a five-course seasonal menu inspired by the Black diaspora, with dishes such as potlikker consomme and oxtail pate en croute described in the opening report.

Price: $125 tasting menu; optional $65 wine pairing  
Schedule: Wednesday-Saturday, 5:00 PM-11:00 PM; official site describes two seatings nightly  
Conditions: Small 14-seat dining room; reservations strongly implied; official fetched text did not state tax/tip/service charges.  
From: 2026-09-09  
Opened: 2026-09-09  
Published: 2026-08-27  
[Source](https://ny.eater.com/news/413465/creola-nyc-restaurant-open-lana-lagomarsini-upper-east-side-tasting-menu)

### Bar CC opens as a walk-in cocktail-and-bar-food spinoff by Crane Club partners

New openings — Bar CC

New bar from Crane Club partners Melissa Rodriguez and Jeff Katz with former Overstory beverage director Harrison Ginsberg; cocktails include dirty CC Martini, tequila-mezcal highball, and clarified milk punch, with a burger, chicken medianoche, and caviar potato chips.

Conditions: Eater states Bar CC is walk-in only; exact hours, prices, and service-charge details remain unverified from an official page. Source: https://ny.eater.com/news/413619/nyc-restaurant-news-september-2026

Schedule: Lunch page exists; exact lunch days/hours not visible in fetched text

### Motek Flatiron $5 Hour seating window

Deals & happy hours — Motek

Official homepage confirms Motek's $5 Hour is active and specifically names a Flatiron $5 Hour menu, but the linked NYC PDF was effectively image/PDF data and no menu-item text could be extracted. Useful as a timing/seating lead, not proof that every item is $5.
